\"01_vocabulary.json\"\nPURPOSE_A_PATH = ROOT / \"contracts\" / \"purpose_a.json\"\n\nLEGAL_SUFFIXES = (\n    \"limited\",\n    \"ltd\",\n    \"incorporated\",\n    \"inc\",\n    \"llc\",\n    \"gmbh\",\n    \"company\",\n    \"co\",\n    \"corp\",\n    \"corporation\",\n    \"lp\",\n    \"llp\",\n    \"plc\",\n)\n\n\ndef normalize_name(name: str) -> str:\n    text = name.lower()\n    text = re.sub(r\"[.,'\\\"()]\", \" \", text)\n    text = re.sub(r\"\\blimited\\b\", \"ltd\", text)\n    text = re.sub(r\"\\bincorporated\\b\", \"inc\", text)\n    text = re.sub(r\"\\bcompany\\b\", \"co\", name_tokens(name: str) -> set[str]:\n    tokens = {token for token in normalize_name(name).split() if token not in LEGAL_SUFFIXES}\n    return {token for token in tokens if len(token) > 2}\n\n\ndef tokens_overlap(left: str, right: str) -> bool:\n    left_tokens = name_tokens(left)\n    right_tokens = name_tokens(right)\n    return bool(left_tokens & right_tokens)\n\n\ndef          expiry_date = re.sub(r\"\\s*\\(.*\\)$\", \"\", expiry_date).strip()\n\n    if stem.startswith(\"SOW-\"):\n        document_kind = \"SOW\"\n    elif stem.startswith(\"MSA-\"):\n pairs.add(oriented_pair(crm_id, billing_id))\n                pairs.add(oriented_pair(billing_id, contract_id))\n\n        commercial_names = [crm[\"account_name\"], *[contract[\"counterparty\"] for
